 INTEGRATE • GO GREEN • 融 合 • 綠 色
CLEAN ENERGY • 潔 淨 能 源
        ANNUAL REPORT 年報 2022
                                 合作共享 開放融合
  昆侖能源有限公司的企業文化有著自身獨特的優勢，既傳承了石油精神的優秀文化內核，又面向市場、
       面向國際，的開放心態，堅持創新驅動，積極踐行社會責任，走綠色發展之路。
    The corporate culture of Kunlun Energy Company Limited has its own unique advantages. Inheriting the fine
       cultural core of the petroleum spirit, it’s oriented towards the market and international arena. With an
open mind for respect, equality, mutual benefit and win-win, we insist on an innovation-driven and green development
                              path, and actively fulfill our corporate social responsibility.
                      低碳經濟 綠色發展
      追求零傷害、零污染、零事故、品質零缺陷、生產降能耗，在品質、健康、
            安全與環境管理方面達到國內同行業先進水準
  Pursue zero harm, zero pollution, zero accident, zero quality defect and production energy
consumption reduction, and achieve a domestically advanced level in quality, health, safety and
                        environmental management of the same trade
                     清潔能源 安全環保
        昆侖能源把綠色作為發展之路，推動戰略接替，加快由天然氣經銷商
           向國際知名、國內一流綠色能源綜合供應商的轉型。
   To promote green development, the Group will smooth the different business strategy,
and expedite its transformation from a natural gas distributor into an internationally renowned
                   and China’s first-class integrated green energy provider.

Notes:                                                                                             附註：
(1)    Net profit margin is profit for the year from continuing operations divided by              (1) 淨利潤率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持續經營
       revenue from continuing operations.                                                             業務收入 。
(2)    Return on total assets is profit for the year from continuing operations divided            (2) 總資產回報率指持續經營業務年內溢利除以持續
       by average total assets from continuing operations.                                             經營業務平均資產總額 。
(3)    Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense and             (3) 淨資本回報率指持續經營業務除所得稅費用及利
       interest from continuing operations divided by total assets less current liabilities            息前溢利除以持續經營業務總資產減流動負債 。
       from continuing operations.
(4)    Liabilities-to EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities from continuing operations    (4)    負債對EBITDA倍數指持續經營業務總負債除以利
       divided by EBITDA.                                                                                 息 、稅項 、折舊及攤銷前盈利 。
(5)    Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings            (5)    淨資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總
       and lease liabilities divided by total equity.                                                     權益 。
(6)    Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings, and lease             (6)    資本負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總權
       liabilities divided by the sum of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings and lease              益 、計息借貸與租賃負債之和 。
       liability.
(7)    Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings and            (7)    資產負債率指計息借貸與租賃負債之和除以總資
       lease liabilities divided by total assets.                                                         產。
(8)    Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of                  (8)    平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔持續經營
       borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing from                        業務總借貸比例 ，然後加總 。
       continuing operations.
(9)    Due to certain Group’s businesses are classified as discontinued operations in              (9)    由於2020年本集團部份業務已分類為已終止持續
       2020. The financial information of the Group has been restated.                                    經營業務 。本集團的財務資料已重列 。
(10)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the potential              (10)   本集團財務資料未就有意出售已終止持續經營業
       disposal of discontinued operations as the directors consider that the unrestated                  務進行重列 ，原因是董事認為未經重列的財務資
       financial information is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of the                       料更適合同比比較本集團業務營運的變化情況 。
       change in the Group’s business operation.
(11)   Core profit before income tax expense is defined as profit before income tax                (11)   除所得稅費用前核心溢利指不包括物業 、廠房及
       expense excluding impairment losses on property, plant and equipment, intangible                   設備 、無形資產減值虧損及匯兌收益╱虧損之除
       assets and exchange gains/losses.                                                                  所得稅費用前溢利 。
(12)   Core profit attributable to shareholders of the Company is defined as profit                (12)   本公司股東應佔核心溢利指不包括本公司股東應
       attributable to shareholders of the Company excluding impairment losses on                         佔之物業 、廠房及設備 、無形資產減值虧損及匯
       property, plant and equipment, intangible assets and exchange gains/losses                         兌收益╱虧損及其有關稅務影響之本公司股東應
       attributable to shareholders of the Company and its related tax effect.                            佔溢利 。

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告




           Fu Bin 付斌
        Chairman and
     Executive Director
       主席兼執行董事




Respected shareholders:                                                  尊敬的各位股東：
For the year ending 31 December 2022 (“the Year”), Kunlun Energy         截至2022年12月31日止年度（以下簡稱「本

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論與分析

         During the year, although the Group was affected by factors such as
         high international gas prices and the market change, we still insisted
         on making active response, focusing on developing the natural
         gas sales market and implementing accurate marketing to realise
         continuous growth in our overall performance. Profit before income
         tax expense was approximately RMB11,392 million, representing an
         increase of 7.7% as compared with RMB10,576 million for the last
         year. Profit attributable to shareholders of the Company for the Year
         was approximately RMB5,228 million, representing an increase of
         1.7% as compared with RMB5,141 million for the last year.
         本年度，本集團雖受國際氣價高企、市場變化等因素影響，但仍堅持積
         極應對，著力開拓天然氣銷售市場並實施精準行銷，實現整體業績持續增
         長。各業務板塊均取得了良好業績，實現之除所得稅費用前溢利約為人民
         幣11,392百萬元，比去年金額人民幣10,576百萬元增加7.7%。於本年度本
         公司股東應佔溢利約為人民幣5,228百萬元，比去年金額人民幣5,141百萬
         元增加1.7%。

DIVIDEND                                                                   股息
Dividend Policy                                                            股息政策
The dividend policy of the Company allows shareholders to share the        本公司之股息政策允許股東分享本公司的利
Company’s profits and also enables the Company to retain sufficient        潤 ，同時亦可為本公司的未來發展保留足夠
reserves for its future development. Under the circumstances that          的儲備 。在本公司盈利且不影響本集團正常
the Company makes profits and has no effect on the Group’s normal          經營的情況下 ，本公司擬以年度股息形式與
business operation, the Company intends to share its profits with          股東分享溢利 ，每年派息總額不少於本公司
its shareholders in the form of annual dividend, which shall be in a       股東應佔本集團合併年度溢利之25% ，惟需
total distribution amount of not less than 25% of the Group’s annual       受以下「考慮因素」所限 。
consolidated profit attributable to the shareholders of the Company,
on the condition that it shall be subject to the following “Criteria”.

Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                         會議常規及操守

Notices of regular Board meetings are served to all Directors at least    董事會定期會議通知至少於會議前14日向全

terms of reference.

financial position of the Company and to enable them to make informed     況 ，並能夠在掌握有關資料之情況下作出決
decisions. The Board and each Director also have separate and             定 。需要時 ，董事會及每名董事亦有個別及
independent access to the senior management whenever necessary.           獨立途徑接觸高級管理人員 。

The Chairman, Chief Executive Officer, President, Chief Financial         需要時，主席、行政總裁、總裁、財務總
Officer and Company Secretary will attend Board and committee             監及公司秘書將出席董事會及委員會會議 ，

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                         業務回顧

          ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are               計估計之修訂僅影響估計修訂期

3   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                    3   主要會計政策
    (a) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                         (a) 附屬公司及非控制性權
                                                                               益
        Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                附屬公司為由本集團控制之實
        Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has                   體。當本集團因參與該實體之
        rights, to variable returns from its involvement with the             營運而獲得或有權享有其可變回
        entity and has the ability to affect those returns through            報 ，並能夠運用其對該實體之權
        its power over the entity. When assessing whether the                 力影響上述回報 ，本集團即對該
        Group has power, only substantive rights (held by the                 實體擁有控制權 。評估本集團是
        Group and other parties) are considered.                              否有權力時 ，僅考慮（本集團及其
                                                                              他方持有的）實質權利 。

        Subsidiaries are consolidated into the consolidated                   於附屬公司的投資自控制權開始
        financial statements from the date that control commences             當日起至控制權終止當日止綜合
        until the date that control ceases. Intra-group balances,             計入綜合財務報表 。集團內部結
        transactions and cash flows and any unrealised profits                餘、交易及現金流量以及集團
        arising from intra- group transactions are eliminated in              內部交易所產生之任何未變現溢
        full in preparing the consolidated financial statements.              利於編製綜合財務報表時悉數對
        Unrealised losses resulting from intra-group transactions             銷 。集團內部交易所產生之未變
        are eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains but                現虧損按與未變現收益相同的方
        only to the extent that there is no evidence of impairment.           式對銷 ，但只限於沒有證據顯示

        Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed                 附屬公司的會計政策已作出必要
        where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies               變動 ，以確保與本集團採納的政
        adopted by the Group                                                  策一致 。

        Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated           非控制性權益在綜合財務狀況表

        the Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of              非控制性權益於綜合全面收益表
        the Group are presented on the face of the consolidated               內呈列 ，作為該年度損益總額及
        statement of comprehensive income as an allocation of                 全面收益總額於本公司非控制性
        the total profit or loss and total comprehensive income               權益與權益股東之間之分配 。
        for the year between non-controlling interests and the
        equity shareholders of the Company.

        Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that                 本集團於附屬公司權益之變動 ，
        do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as               如無導致失去控制權 ，則入賬列

        the amounts of controlling and non-controlling interests              股及非控制性權益金額則予以調

    (h) Leased assets                                                        (h) 租賃資產
        At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the              本集團於合約開始時評估合約是
        contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains,          否屬於（或包含）租賃 。倘合約為
        a lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use            換取代價而轉移已識別資產於一
        of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for             段時間內之使用控制權 ，則合約
        consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                   屬於（或包含）租賃 。倘客戶既有
        has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset            權指示已識別資產之使用又有權
        and to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits                獲取有關使用帶來之絕大部分經
        from that use.                                                          濟利益 ，則控制權已被轉移 。

        (i)   As a lessee                                                       (i)   作為承租人

              Where the contract contains lease components and                        對於包含租賃部分及非租賃
              non-lease components, the Group has elected not                         部分之合約 ，本集團已選擇
              to separate non-lease components and accounts                           不將非租賃部分分開處理 ，
              for each lease component and any associated non-                        而是就所有租賃將各租賃部
              lease components as a single lease component for                        分及任何相關之非租賃部分
              all leases.                                                             作為單一租賃部分入賬 。

              At the lease commencement date, the Group                               本集團於租賃開始日期確認
              recognises a right-of-use asset and a lease liability,                  使用權資產及租賃負債 ，惟
              except for short-term leases that have a lease term                     租 期 為 12 個 月 或 以 下 之 短
              of 12 months or less and leases of low-value assets.                    期租賃或低價值資產租賃除
              When the Group enters into a lease in respect of                        外 。當本集團就低價值資產
              a low-value asset, the Group decides whether to                         訂立租賃時 ，本集團會按租
              capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease basis. The                     賃之個別情況決定是否將租
              lease payments associated with those leases which                       賃資本化。與該等並未資
              are not capitalised are recognised as an expense on                     本化之租賃相關之租賃付款
              a systematic basis over the lease term.                                 於租期內有系統地確認為開

            value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of              變現淨值為於日常業務過程中之

            Note 3(l)(i) and are reclassified to receivables when the right              在代價權利成為無條件後重新分

            related revenue.

           Borrowings are measured initially at fair value less                  借貸初步按公平值減交易成本計
           transaction costs. Subsequent to initial recognition,                 量 。於初步確認後 ，計息借貸則

           using the effective interest method.

           General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable            直接來自收購 、興建或生產合資
           to the acquisition, construction or production of qualifying          格資產且該等資產需經較長時間
           assets, which are assets that necessarily take a substantial          方會達至預定可使用或出售狀態
           period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale,           之一般及特定借貸成本 ，計入該
           are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as             等資產之成本 ，直至大致上達至
           the assets are substantially ready for their intended use             其預定可使用或出售狀態為止 。
           or sale.

           All other borrowing costs are recognised in the profit or             所有其他借貸成本於其產生期間
           loss in the period in which they are incurred.                        於損益中確認 。

           reporting period.                                                     則作別論 。

        (b) Credit risk                                                                 (b) 信貸風險

             financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk                            蒙受財務損失之風險 。本集
             arises primarily from accounts receivable.                                      團之信貸風險主要來自於應
                                                                                             收賬款 。

             As a majority of the Group’s cash at bank and                                   本集團大部分銀行現金及定
             time deposits were placed with major state-owned                                期存款存放於中國內地的主
             banks in Mainland China, major banks in Hong Kong,                              要國有銀行、香港的主要
             and financial institutions within the CNPC Group,                               銀行及中油集團內的金融機
             management believes that the credit risk is low.                                構 ，管理層認為信貸風險較
                                                                                             低。

                ECL rates are based on actual loss experience                     預期信貸虧損率基於過去五
                over the past five years. These rates are adjusted                年之實際虧損經驗計算 。該
                to reflect differences between economic conditions                等比率根據歷史數據收集期
                during the period over which the historic data has                間之經濟狀況 、現況與本集
                been collected, current conditions and the Group’s                團所認為之應收賬款預期存

      4.1 Financial risk factors (Continued)                                       4.1 財務風險因素（續）
           (c) Liquidity risk                                                        (c) 流動資金風險

                Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter               流動資金風險為本集團難以
                difficulty in meeting obligations associated with                      履行須提供現金或另一金融
                financial liabilities that are settled by delivering cash              資產以清償財務負債之相關
                or another financial asset.                                            責任之風險 。

                The Group’s liquidity risk management involves                         本集團之流動資金風險管理
                maintaining sufficient cash and cash equivalents and                   涉及維持充足現金及現金等
                availability of funding through an adequate amount                     值項目 ，以及透過適當之授
                of committed credit facilities. Management prepares                    信額度提供可用資金 。管理
                monthly cash flow budget to ensure that the Group                      層每月編製現金流量預算以
                will always have sufficient liquidity to meet its financial            確保本集團擁有足夠的流動
                obligations as they fall due. The Group arranges and                   資金履行到期財務義務 。本
                negotiates financing with financial institutions and                   集團安排並與金融機構進行
                maintains a certain level of standby credit facilities                 融資磋商 ，保持一定水平的
                to reduce the Group’s liquidity risk.                                  備用授信額度以降低本集團
                                                                                       的流動資金風險 。

                Based on the existing level of gearing and continued                   鑒於目前資本負債率水平以
                access to funding, the Group believes that its liquidity               及持續獲得融資 ，本集團相
                risk is not material.                                                  信其流動資金風險並不高 。

                Analysis of the Group’s financial liabilities based on                 根據綜合財務狀況表日至合
                the remaining period at the date of the consolidated                   約到期日餘下期間對本集團
                statement of financial position to the contractual                     金融負債所作之分析列示於
                maturity dates are presented in Notes 29 and 30.                       附註29及30。

                because of changes in market interest rates. The                       利率改變而波動所帶來的風
                Group’s interest rate risk arises primarily from                       險 。本集團之利率風險主要
                borrowings. Borrowings issued at variable rates and                    源自借貸 。按浮動利率及固
                fixed rates expose the Group to cash flow interest                     定利率發出之借貸分別令本

      4.2 Capital risk management                                             4.2 資本風險管理
           The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                  本集團管理資本之目標是保障其
           safeguard its ability to continue as a going concern,                持續經營之能力 ，以優化股東回
           optimise returns for shareholders and to minimise its cost           報及降低其資本成本 。於達至本
           of capital. In meeting its objectives of managing capital,           集團之資本管理目標方面 ，本集
           the Group may issue new shares, adjust its debt levels or            團可發行新股份 、調整其負債水
           the mix between short-term and long-term borrowings.                 平或短期與長期借貸之間的組合 。

           The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing               本集團根據資本負債率（以計息借
           ratio which is calculated as the sum of interest-bearing             貸及租賃負債之和除以總權益 、
           borrowings and lease liabilities divided by the sum of total         計息借貸及租賃負債之和計算）監
           equity, interest-bearing borrowings and lease liabilities.           察 資 本 。 於 2022 年 12 月 31 日 之
           The gearing ratio at 31 December 2022 is 24.26%                      資本負債率為24.26%( 2021年：
           (2021:24.92%).                                                       24.92%）。

           There were no changes in the management’s approach to                管理層對本集團的資本管理方針
           capital management of the Group during the year. Neither             在年內並無變更 。本公司及其附
           the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject to               屬公司均不受來自外部的資本要
           externally imposed capital requirements.                             求所限 。

        (a) Financial assets measured at fair value                           (a) 按公平值計量之金融
                                                                                  資產

             Fair value hierarchy                                                  公平值層級

             The following table presents the fair value of the                    下表呈列本集團金融工具之
             Group’s financial instruments measured at the end of                  公平值 ，該等金融工具於報
             the reporting period on a recurring basis, categorised                告期末按經常性基準計量 ，
             into the three-level fair value hierarchy. The level                  並分類為公平值計量所界定
             into which a fair value measurement is classified                     之三級公平值架構 。將公平
             is determined with reference to the observability                     值計量分類之層級乃經參考
             and significance of the inputs used in the valuation                  如下估值方法所用數據之可
             technique as follows:                                                 觀察性及重要性後釐定：

             –    Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using                    –    第 一 級 估 值： 使 用 於
                  unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for                        計量日同等資產或負
                  identical assets or liabilities at the measurement                    債於活躍市場之未經
                  date.                                                                 調整報價計量之公平
                                                                                        值。

             –    Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using                    –    第 二 級 估 值： 使 用 估
                  valuation techniques which maximise the use                           值技術計量之公平
                  of observable market data and rely as little                          值 ，該等估值技術盡
                  as possible on entity-specific estimates. If                          量利用可觀察市場數
                  all significant inputs required to fair value an                      據而極少依賴實體的
                  instrument are observable, the instrument is                          特定估計 。倘工具的
                  included in level 2.                                                  公平值所需所有重大
                                                                                        輸入數據均為可觀察
                                                                                        數據 ，則該工具計入
                                                                                        第二級 。

             –    Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                    –    第 三 級 估 值： 使 用 重
                  significant unobservable inputs.                                      大不可觀察輸入數據
                                                                                        計量之公平值 。

   (iii) Equipment and machinery                                            (iii) 設備及機器

          The Group leases equipment and machinery under leases                    本集團根據於十六年至二十年內

      senior executive management and Executive Directors of the
      Company.

      The Group organises its business around products and                本集團按產品及服務組織其業務 。就產
      services. From the products and services perspective, the           品及服務而言 ，本集團廣泛從事一系列
      Group is engaged in a broad range of oil and gas related            石油和天然氣相關業務 ，其收入來自四
      activities and derives its revenue from its four operating          個營運板塊：天然氣銷售 、LPG銷售 、
      segments: Natural Gas Sales, Sales of LPG, LNG Processing           LNG加工與儲運及勘探與生產 。
      and Terminal and Exploration and Production.

      The Natural Gas Sales segment is engaged in the retail sales,       天然氣銷售板塊從事不同天然氣產品的
      distribution and trading of various natural gas products. The       零售及分銷與貿易 。LPG銷售板塊從事
      Sales of LPG segment is engaged in the wholesales and               不同LPG產品的批發與零售 。LNG加工
      retail sales of various LPG products. LNG Processing and            與儲運板塊從事LNG加工 、卸載 、儲
      Terminal segment is engaged in the processing, unloading,           存 、氣化及裝車 。勘探與生產板塊從
      storage, gasification and entrucking of LNG. The Exploration        事原油及天然氣之勘探 、開發 、生產
      and Production segment is engaged in the exploration,               和銷售 。
      development, production and sales of crude oil and natural
      gas.

      The Group’s most senior executive management and                    本集團最高管理層及執行董事根據各板
      Executive Directors assess the performance of the operating         塊之除所得稅費用 、應佔聯營公司及合
      segments based on each segment’s profit/(loss) before               資企業之溢利減虧損前之溢利╱（虧損）
      income tax expense, and share of profits less losses of             評估經營板塊之表現（「板塊業績」）。
      associates and joint ventures (“segment results”).

      financial assets, investments in associates and joint ventures.     金融資產 、於聯營公司及合資企業之投
                                                                          資。

      general and administrative expenses and interest expenses           損）淨額 、公司層面產生之一般性和管
      incurred at corporate level.                                        理費用及利息支出 。

Definition:                                                           釋義：
The petroleum reserves are classified as follows:                     石油儲量分類如下：

Proved oil and gas reserves – Proved oil and gas reserves are         探明油氣儲量 －探明油氣儲量為自給定日期
those quantities of oil and gas, which by analysis of geoscience      至合同約定權利到期日（除非有證據合理保證
and engineering data, can be estimated with reasonable certainty      該權利能夠得到延期），透過地球科學和工程
to be economically producible – from a given date forward, from       數據的分析 ，採用確定性或概率性評估 ，以
known reservoirs, and under existing economic conditions, operating   現有經濟 、作業和政府管制條件 ，可以合理
methods, and government regulations – prior to the time at which      確定已知油氣藏經濟可採油氣的數量 。項目
contracts providing the right to operate expire, unless evidence      須已開始萃取烴或作業者須合理確定其將於
indicates that renewal is reasonably certain, regardless of whether   合理時間內開始項目 。
deterministic or probabilistic methods are used for the estimation.
The project to extract the hydrocarbons must have commenced or
the operator must be reasonably certain that it will commence the
project within a reasonable time.

Developed oil and gas reserves – Developed oil and gas reserves               已開發油氣儲量 －已開發油氣儲量為預期可
are reserves of any category that can be expected to be recovered:            透過以下方式開採的儲量：

(i)     Through existing wells with existing equipment and operating          (i)     利用現有設備和作業方法 ，或者開採儲
        methods or in which the cost of the required equipment is                     量所需的開發設備成本明顯低於鑽探一
        relatively minor compared to the cost of a new well; and                      口新井所需成本 ，可從現有油氣井中進
                                                                                      行開採的儲量；及

(ii)    Through installed extraction equipment and infrastructure             (ii)    當通過除油氣井開採外的其他方式進行
        operational at the time of the reserves estimate if the extraction            開採 ，利用儲量估計時點已安裝的開採
        is by means not involving a well.                                             設備和基礎設施可開採的儲量 。

Undeveloped oil and gas reserves – Undeveloped oil and gas reserves           未開發油氣儲量 －未開發油氣儲量指預期可
are reserves of any category that are expected to be recovered from           從未鑽區域的新井中採出 ，或再完井需要較
new wells on undrilled acreage, or from existing wells where a relatively     高支出的現有井中採出的儲量 。
major expenditure is required from recompletion.

(i)     Reserves on undrilled acreage shall be limited to those directly      (i)     未鑽區域的儲量僅限於直接扣除開發間
        offsetting development spacing areas that are reasonably                      距區域 ，有一定把握在鑽後能採油 ，
        certain of production when drilled, unless evidence using                     除非證實運用可靠技術能有一定把握在
        reliable technology exists that establishes reasonable certainty              更遠距離在經濟上可生產 。
        of economic producibility at greater distances.

(ii)    Undrilled locations can be classified as having undeveloped           (ii)    倘已採納的開發方案表明計劃在五年內
        reserves only if a development plan has been adopted indicating               （在特定環境下須較長時間者除外）鑽
        that they are scheduled to be drilled within five years, unless the           井 ，未鑽區域方可分類為擁有未開發儲
        specific circumstances, justify a longer time.                                量。

(iii)   Under no circumstances shall estimates for undeveloped reserves       (iii)   任何未開發儲量的估計均不得包括擬運
        be attributable to any acreage for which an application of fluid              用流體注入或其他改良採油技術的任何
        injection or other improved recovery technique is contemplated,               區域 ，除非該等技術已通過在相同油藏
        unless such techniques have been proved effective by actual                   或類似油藏的實際項目 ，或通過採用可
        projects in the same reservoir or an analogous reservoir, or by               靠技術證實有一定把握的其他證據證明
        other evidence using reliable technology establishing reasonable              為有效 。
        certainty.
